===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1276-1287
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1276 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1277 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1278 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1279 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1280 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1281 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1282 |       } else {
1283 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1284 |       }
1285 |     }
1286 |     return builder.build();
1287 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,CompilerOptions,JSTypeRegistry,SourceMap,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 654-691
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to JS code
   */
Method code:
654 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
655 |       CompilerOptions options, JSTypeRegistry registry,
656 |       SourceMap sourceMap,  boolean tagAsStrict) {
657 |     Preconditions.checkState(options.sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
658 | 
659 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
660 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
661 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
662 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
663 |             options.lineBreak,
664 |             options.preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile,
665 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
666 |             createSourceMap,
667 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel)
668 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
669 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
670 |             createSourceMap,
671 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel);
672 |     CodeGenerator cg =
673 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
674 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, options, registry)
675 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, options);
676 | 
677 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
678 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
679 |     }
680 | 
681 |     cg.add(root);
682 |     mcp.endFile();
683 | 
684 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
685 | 
686 |     if (createSourceMap) {
687 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
688 |     }
689 | 
690 |     return code;
691 |   }

===== M0023 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 92-152
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
93 |     switch (n.getType()) {
94 |       case Token.LABEL:
95 |         validateLabel(n);
96 |         return;
97 |       case Token.BLOCK:
98 |         validateBlock(n);
99 |         return;
100 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
101 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
102 |         return;
103 |       case Token.WITH:
104 |         validateWith(n);
105 |         return;
106 |       case Token.FOR:
107 |         validateFor(n);
108 |         return;
109 |       case Token.WHILE:
110 |         validateWhile(n);
111 |         return;
112 |       case Token.DO:
113 |         validateDo(n);
114 |         return;
115 |       case Token.SWITCH:
116 |         validateSwitch(n);
117 |         return;
118 |       case Token.IF:
119 |         validateIf(n);
120 |         return;
121 |       case Token.VAR:
122 |         validateVar(n);
123 |         return;
124 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
125 |         validateExprStmt(n);
126 |         return;
127 |       case Token.RETURN:
128 |         validateReturn(n);
129 |         return;
130 |       case Token.THROW:
131 |         validateThrow(n);
132 |         return;
133 |       case Token.TRY:
134 |         validateTry(n);
135 |         return;
136 |       case Token.BREAK:
137 |         validateBreak(n);
138 |         return;
139 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
140 |         validateContinue(n);
141 |         return;
142 |       case Token.EMPTY:
143 |         validateChildless(n);
144 |         return;
145 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
146 |         validateChildless(n);
147 |         return;
148 |       default:
149 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
150 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
151 |     }
152 |   }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformBlock(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 224-239
Documentation:

Method code:
224 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
225 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
226 |     if (!irNode.isBlock()) {
227 |       if (irNode.isEmpty()) {
228 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
229 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
230 |       } else {
231 |         Node newBlock = newNode(Token.BLOCK, irNode);
232 |         newBlock.setLineno(irNode.getLineno());
233 |         newBlock.setCharno(irNode.getCharno());
234 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newBlock, node);
235 |         irNode = newBlock;
236 |       }
237 |     }
238 |     return irNode;
239 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 393-423
Documentation:
/**
   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources
   * are in modules.
   */
Method code:
393 |   public <T extends SourceFile> void initModules(
394 |       List<T> externs, List<JSModule> modules, CompilerOptions options) {
395 |     initOptions(options);
396 | 
397 |     checkFirstModule(modules);
398 |     fillEmptyModules(modules);
399 | 
400 |     this.externs = makeCompilerInput(externs, true);
401 | 
402 |     // Generate the module graph, and report any errors in the module
403 |     // specification as errors.
404 |     this.modules = modules;
405 |     if (modules.size() > 1) {
406 |       try {
407 |         this.moduleGraph = new JSModuleGraph(modules);
408 |       } catch (JSModuleGraph.ModuleDependenceException e) {
409 |         // problems with the module format.  Report as an error.  The
410 |         // message gives all details.
411 |         report(JSError.make(MODULE_DEPENDENCY_ERROR,
412 |                 e.getModule().getName(), e.getDependentModule().getName()));
413 |         return;
414 |       }
415 |     } else {
416 |       this.moduleGraph = null;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     this.inputs = getAllInputsFromModules(modules);
420 |     initBasedOnOptions();
421 | 
422 |     initInputsByIdMap();
423 |   }

262 |   private void initializeBuiltInTypes() {
263 |     // These locals shouldn't be all caps.

273 |     StringType STRING_TYPE = new StringType(this);
274 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.STRING_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
275 | 
276 |     UnknownType UNKNOWN_TYPE = new UnknownType(this, false);
277 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE, UNKNOWN_TYPE);
278 |     UnknownType checkedUnknownType = new UnknownType(this, true);
279 |     registerNativeType(
280 |         JSTypeNative.CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE, checkedUnknownType);

282 |     VoidType VOID_TYPE = new VoidType(this);
283 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.VOID_TYPE, VOID_TYPE);
284 | 
285 |     AllType ALL_TYPE = new AllType(this);
286 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.ALL_TYPE, ALL_TYPE);
287 | 
288 |     // Top Level Prototype (the One)
289 |     // The initializations of TOP_LEVEL_PROTOTYPE and OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE
290 |     // use each other's results, so at least one of them will get null
291 |     // instead of an actual type; however, this seems to be benign.
292 |     PrototypeObjectType TOP_LEVEL_PROTOTYPE =
293 |         new PrototypeObjectType(this, null, null, true, null);
294 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.TOP_LEVEL_PROTOTYPE, TOP_LEVEL_PROTOTYPE);

298 |         new FunctionType(this, "Object", null,
299 |             createArrowType(createOptionalParameters(ALL_TYPE), UNKNOWN_TYPE),
300 |             null,

302 |                 OBJECT_INDEX_TEMPLATE, OBJECT_ELEMENT_TEMPLATE), null),
303 |             true, true);
304 | 
305 |     OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE.setPrototype(TOP_LEVEL_PROTOTYPE, null);
306 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE, OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE);
307 | 
308 |     ObjectType OBJECT_TYPE = OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE.getInstanceType();
309 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE, OBJECT_TYPE);
310 | 
311 |     ObjectType OBJECT_PROTOTYPE = OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE.getPrototype();
312 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_PROTOTYPE, OBJECT_PROTOTYPE);
313 | 
314 |     // Function
315 |     FunctionType FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE =
316 |         new FunctionType(this, "Function", null,
317 |             createArrowType(
318 |                 createParametersWithVarArgs(ALL_TYPE), UNKNOWN_TYPE),
319 |             null, null, true, true);
320 |     FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE.setPrototypeBasedOn(OBJECT_TYPE);

===== M0039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 179-202
Documentation:

Method code:
179 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
180 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
181 |                                    String sourceString,
182 |                                    Config config,
183 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
184 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
185 |         config, errorReporter);
186 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
187 | 
188 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
189 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
190 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
191 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {
192 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
193 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
194 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
195 |         }
196 |       }
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
200 | 
201 |     return irNode;
202 |   }

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
428 |   boolean setPrototype(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
429 |     if (prototype == null) {
430 |       return false;
431 |     }

433 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
434 |       return false;
435 |     }
436 |     return setPrototypeNoCheck(prototype, propertyNode);
437 |   }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 379-404
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticeable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
380 |         final String defaultResource
381 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
382 | 
383 |         Locale locale = Locale.getDefault();
384 | 
385 |         // ResourceBundle does caching.
386 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
387 | 
388 |         String formatString;
389 |         try {
390 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
391 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
392 |             throw new RuntimeException
393 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
394 |         }
395 | 
396 |         /*
397 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
398 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
399 |          * single 's.
400 |          */
401 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
402 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
403 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
404 |     }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformParameter(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 383-394
Documentation:
/**
   * Parameter NAMEs are special, because they can have inline type docs
   * attached.
   *
   * function f(/** string &#42;/ x) {}
   * annotates 'x' as a string.
   *
   * @see http://code.google.com/p/jsdoc-toolkit/wiki/InlineDocs
   */
Method code:
383 |   private Node transformParameter(AstNode node) {
384 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
385 |     Comment comment = node.getJsDocNode();
386 |     if (comment != null) {
387 |       JSDocInfo info = parseInlineTypeDoc(comment, irNode);
388 |       if (info != null) {
389 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(info);
390 |       }
391 |     }
392 |     setSourceInfo(irNode, node);
393 |     return irNode;
394 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatementParent(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1755-1768
Documentation:

Method code:
1755 |   static boolean isStatementParent(Node parent) {
1756 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1757 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1758 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1759 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1760 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1761 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1762 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1763 |       case Token.LABEL:
1764 |         return true;
1765 |       default:
1766 |         return false;
1767 |     }
1768 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1169-1244
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1169 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1170 |     switch (type) {
1171 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1183 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1184 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1185 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1186 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1187 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1188 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1189 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1190 |       case Token.EQ:
1191 |       case Token.NE:
1192 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1193 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1194 |       case Token.LT:
1195 |       case Token.GT:
1196 |       case Token.LE:
1197 |       case Token.GE:
1198 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1199 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1200 |       case Token.LSH:
1201 |       case Token.RSH:
1202 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1203 |       case Token.SUB:
1204 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1205 |       case Token.MUL:
1206 |       case Token.MOD:
1207 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1208 |       case Token.INC:
1209 |       case Token.DEC:
1210 |       case Token.NEW:
1211 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1212 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1213 |       case Token.VOID:
1214 |       case Token.NOT:
1215 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1216 |       case Token.POS:
1217 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1218 | 
1219 |       case Token.CALL:
1220 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1221 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1222 |       // Data values
1223 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1224 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1225 |       case Token.FALSE:
1226 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1227 |       case Token.NAME:
1228 |       case Token.NULL:
1229 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1230 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1231 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1232 |       case Token.STRING:
1233 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
1234 |       case Token.THIS:
1235 |       case Token.TRUE:
1236 |         return 15;
1237 |       case Token.CAST:
1238 |         return 16;
1239 | 
1240 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1241 |                                Token.name(type) +
1242 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1243 |     }
1244 |   }

===== M0083 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1495-1544
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1495 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1496 |     if (type != node.getType()
1497 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1498 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1499 |       return false;
1500 |     }
1501 | 
1502 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1503 |       return false;
1504 |     }
1505 | 
1506 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1507 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1508 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1509 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1510 |         return false;
1511 |       }
1512 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1513 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1514 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1515 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1516 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1517 |           return false;
1518 |         }
1519 |       }
1520 | 
1521 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1522 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1523 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1524 |         return false;
1525 |       }
1526 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1527 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1528 |         return false;
1529 |       }
1530 |     }
1531 | 
1532 |     if (recurse) {
1533 |       Node n, n2;
1534 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1535 |            n != null;
1536 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1537 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1538 |           return false;
1539 |         }
1540 |       }
1541 |     }
1542 | 
1543 |     return true;
1544 |   }

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 648-660
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
648 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
649 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
650 |     if (typeSet == null) {
651 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
652 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
653 |     }
654 | 
655 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
656 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
657 | 
658 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
659 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
660 |   }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 103-756
Documentation:

Method code:
103 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
104 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
105 |       return;
106 |     }
107 | 
108 |     int type = n.getType();
109 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
110 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
111 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
112 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
113 | 
114 |     // Handle all binary operators
115 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(
117 |           childCount == 2,
118 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
119 |           opstr, childCount);
120 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
121 | 
122 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
123 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
124 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
125 | 
126 |       // Handle associativity.
127 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
128 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
129 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
130 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
131 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
132 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
133 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
134 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
135 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
136 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
137 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
138 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
139 |       } else {
140 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
141 |       }
142 |       return;
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
146 | 
147 |     switch (type) {
148 |       case Token.TRY: {
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
150 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
152 | 
153 |         add("try");
154 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
155 | 
156 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
157 |         // isn't a catch block
158 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
159 |         if (catchblock != null) {
160 |           add(catchblock);
161 |         }
162 | 
163 |         if (childCount == 3) {
164 |           add("finally");
165 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
166 |         }
167 |         break;
168 |       }
169 | 
170 |       case Token.CATCH:
171 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
172 |         add("catch(");
173 |         add(first);
174 |         add(")");
175 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.THROW:
179 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
180 |         add("throw");
181 |         add(first);
182 | 
183 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
184 |         // parse this.
185 |         cc.endStatement(true);
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.RETURN:
189 |         add("return");
190 |         if (childCount == 1) {
191 |           add(first);
192 |         } else {
193 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
194 |         }
195 |         cc.endStatement();
196 |         break;
197 | 
198 |       case Token.VAR:
199 |         if (first != null) {
200 |           add("var ");
201 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 | 
205 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
206 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
207 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.NAME:
211 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
212 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
213 |         } else {
214 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
215 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
216 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
217 |           if (first.isComma()) {
218 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
219 |           } else {
220 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
221 |             // precedence.
222 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
228 |         add("[");
229 |         addArrayList(first);
230 |         add("]");
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
234 |         add("(");
235 |         addList(first);
236 |         add(")");
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.COMMA:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
241 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
242 |         break;
243 | 
244 |       case Token.NUMBER:
245 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
246 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
247 |         break;
248 | 
249 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
250 |       case Token.VOID:
251 |       case Token.NOT:
252 |       case Token.BITNOT:
253 |       case Token.POS: {
254 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
255 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
256 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
257 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
258 |         break;
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       case Token.NEG: {
262 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
263 | 
264 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
265 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
266 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
267 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
268 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
269 |         } else {
270 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
271 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         break;
275 |       }
276 | 
277 |       case Token.HOOK: {
278 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
279 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
280 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
281 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
282 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
283 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
284 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
285 |         break;
286 |       }
287 | 
288 |       case Token.REGEXP:
289 |         if (!first.isString() ||
290 |             !last.isString()) {
291 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
292 |         }
293 | 
294 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
295 | 
296 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
297 |         if (childCount == 2) {
298 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
299 |         } else {
300 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
301 |           add(regexp);
302 |         }
303 |         break;
304 | 
305 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
306 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
307 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
308 |         }
309 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
310 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
311 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
312 |           add("(");
313 |         }
314 | 
315 |         add("function");
316 |         add(first);
317 | 
318 |         add(first.getNext());
319 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
320 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
321 | 
322 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
323 |           add(")");
324 |         }
325 |         break;
326 | 
327 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
328 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
329 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
330 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
331 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
332 | 
333 |         // Get methods are unnamed
334 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
335 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
336 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
337 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
338 |           add("get ");
339 |         } else {
340 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
341 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
342 |           add("set ");
343 |         }
344 | 
345 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
346 |         String name = n.getString();
347 |         Node fn = first;
348 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
349 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
350 | 
351 |         // Add the property name.
352 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
353 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
354 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
355 |             // Unicode escaped.
356 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
357 |           add(name);
358 |         } else {
359 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
360 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
361 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
362 |             cc.addNumber(d);
363 |           } else {
364 |             addJsString(n);
365 |           }
366 |         }
367 | 
368 |         add(parameters);
369 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
370 |         break;
371 | 
372 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
373 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
374 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
375 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
376 |         }
377 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
378 |         if (preserveBlock) {
379 |           cc.beginBlock();
380 |         }
381 | 
382 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
383 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
384 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
385 |                 !preserveBlock &&
386 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
387 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
388 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
389 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
390 | 
391 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
392 |           if (c.isVar()) {
393 |             cc.endStatement();
394 |           }
395 | 
396 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
397 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
398 |           }
399 | 
400 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
401 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
402 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
403 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
404 |           }
405 |         }
406 |         if (preserveBlock) {
407 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
408 |         }
409 |         break;
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       case Token.FOR:
413 |         if (childCount == 4) {
414 |           add("for(");
415 |           if (first.isVar()) {
416 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
417 |           } else {
418 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
419 |           }
420 |           add(";");
421 |           add(first.getNext());
422 |           add(";");
423 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
424 |           add(")");
425 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
426 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
427 |         } else {
428 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
429 |           add("for(");
430 |           add(first);
431 |           add("in");
432 |           add(first.getNext());
433 |           add(")");
434 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
435 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
436 |         }
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.DO:
440 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
441 |         add("do");
442 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(last);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         cc.endStatement();
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case Token.WHILE:
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
451 |         add("while(");
452 |         add(first);
453 |         add(")");
454 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
455 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.EMPTY:
459 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
460 |         break;
461 | 
462 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
463 |         Preconditions.checkState(
464 |             childCount == 2,
465 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
466 |         Preconditions.checkState(
467 |             last.isString(),
468 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
469 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
470 |         if (needsParens) {
471 |           add("(");
472 |         }
473 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
474 |         if (needsParens) {
475 |           add(")");
476 |         }
477 |         add(".");
478 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
479 |         break;
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       case Token.GETELEM:
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(
484 |             childCount == 2,
485 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
486 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
487 |         add("[");
488 |         add(first.getNext());
489 |         add("]");
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.WITH:
493 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
494 |         add("with(");
495 |         add(first);
496 |         add(")");
497 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
498 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.INC:
502 |       case Token.DEC: {
503 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
504 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
505 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
506 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
507 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
508 |         if (postProp != 0) {
509 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
510 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
511 |         } else {
512 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
513 |           add(first);
514 |         }
515 |         break;
516 |       }
517 | 
518 |       case Token.CALL:
519 |         // We have two special cases here:
520 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
521 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
522 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
523 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
524 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
525 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
526 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
527 |         // that must be preserved.
528 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
529 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
530 |           add("(0,");
531 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
532 |           add(")");
533 |         } else {
534 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
535 |         }
536 |         add("(");
537 |         addList(first.getNext());
538 |         add(")");
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.IF:
542 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
543 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
544 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
545 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
546 |           cc.beginBlock();
547 |         }
548 | 
549 |         add("if(");
550 |         add(first);
551 |         add(")");
552 | 
553 |         if (hasElse) {
554 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
555 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
556 |           add("else");
557 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
558 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
559 |         } else {
560 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
561 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
562 |         }
563 | 
564 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
565 |           cc.endBlock();
566 |         }
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NULL:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
571 |         cc.addConstant("null");
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.THIS:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
576 |         add("this");
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.FALSE:
580 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
581 |         cc.addConstant("false");
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.TRUE:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         cc.addConstant("true");
587 |         break;
588 | 
589 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
590 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
591 |         add("continue");
592 |         if (childCount == 1) {
593 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
594 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
595 |           }
596 |           add(" ");
597 |           add(first);
598 |         }
599 |         cc.endStatement();
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
603 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
604 |         add("debugger");
605 |         cc.endStatement();
606 |         break;
607 | 
608 |       case Token.BREAK:
609 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
610 |         add("break");
611 |         if (childCount == 1) {
612 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
613 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
614 |           }
615 |           add(" ");
616 |           add(first);
617 |         }
618 |         cc.endStatement();
619 |         break;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
622 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
623 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
624 |         cc.endStatement();
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.NEW:
628 |         add("new ");
629 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
630 | 
631 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
632 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
633 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
634 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
635 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
636 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
637 |         }
638 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
639 | 
640 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
641 |         Node next = first.getNext();
642 |         if (next != null) {
643 |           add("(");
644 |           addList(next);
645 |           add(")");
646 |         }
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
650 |         Preconditions.checkState(
651 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
652 |         addJsString(n);
653 |         break;
654 | 
655 |       case Token.STRING:
656 |         Preconditions.checkState(
657 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
658 |         addJsString(n);
659 |         break;
660 | 
661 |       case Token.DELPROP:
662 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
663 |         add("delete ");
664 |         add(first);
665 |         break;
666 | 
667 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
668 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
669 |         if (needsParens) {
670 |           add("(");
671 |         }
672 |         add("{");
673 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
674 |           if (c != first) {
675 |             cc.listSeparator();
676 |           }
677 | 
678 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
679 |             add(c);
680 |           } else {
681 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
682 |             String key = c.getString();
683 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
684 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
685 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
686 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
687 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
688 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
689 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
690 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
691 |               add(key);
692 |             } else {
693 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
694 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
695 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
696 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
697 |               } else {
698 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
699 |               }
700 |             }
701 |             add(":");
702 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
703 |           }
704 |         }
705 |         add("}");
706 |         if (needsParens) {
707 |           add(")");
708 |         }
709 |         break;
710 |       }
711 | 
712 |       case Token.SWITCH:
713 |         add("switch(");
714 |         add(first);
715 |         add(")");
716 |         cc.beginBlock();
717 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
718 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
723 |         add("case ");
724 |         add(first);
725 |         addCaseBody(last);
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
729 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
730 |         add("default");
731 |         addCaseBody(first);
732 |         break;
733 | 
734 |       case Token.LABEL:
735 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
736 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
737 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
738 |         }
739 |         add(first);
740 |         add(":");
741 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
742 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
743 |         break;
744 | 
745 |       case Token.CAST:
746 |         add("(");
747 |         add(first);
748 |         add(")");
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       default:
752 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
753 |     }
754 | 
755 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
756 |   }

===== M0088 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1182-1190
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1182 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1183 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1184 |       return -1;
1185 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1186 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1187 |     } else {
1188 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1189 |     }
1190 |   }

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 396-421
Documentation:

Method code:
396 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
397 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
398 |     // two cases:
399 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
400 |     // and
401 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
402 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
403 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
404 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
405 |     //
406 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
407 |     //
408 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
409 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
410 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
411 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
412 |     //
413 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
414 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
415 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
416 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
417 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
418 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
419 |     }
420 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
421 |   }

===== M0135 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1221-1223
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1221 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1222 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1223 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getSortedPropTypes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
859 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
860 |     int count = 0;
861 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
862 |       count++;
863 |     }
864 | 
865 |     int[] keys = new int[count];

867 |       count--;
868 |       keys[count] = x.getType();
869 |     }

872 |     return keys;
873 |   }

===== M0153 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

147 |   private boolean shouldRetraverse(Node node) {
148 |     if (retraverseOnChange
149 |         && node.getParent() != null
150 |         && (node.isFunction() || node.isScript())) {
151 |       ScopeState state = traversalState.peek();
152 |       if (state.changed) {

154 |         // when revisiting, only visit the immediate scope

160 |       }
161 |     }
162 |     return false;

===== M0162 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1165-1185
Documentation:

Method code:
1165 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1166 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1167 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1168 |       return s;
1169 |     }
1170 | 
1171 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1172 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1173 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1174 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1175 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1176 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1177 |       // crazy.
1178 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1179 |         sb.append(c);
1180 |       } else {
1181 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1182 |       }
1183 |     }
1184 |     return sb.toString();
1185 |   }

70 |       requiresLineNumbers = false;
71 |     } else if (requiresLineNumbers) {
72 |       if (n.getLineno() == -1) {
73 |         // The tree version of the node is really the best diagnostic
74 |         // info we have to offer here.
75 |         compiler.report(
76 |             t.makeError(n, MISSING_LINE_INFO,
77 |                 n.toStringTree()));
78 |       }
79 |     }
80 |   }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

   * so make a best guess.

563 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
564 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
565 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:

567 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
568 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
569 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
570 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
571 |       case Token.BITAND:
572 |       case Token.BITOR:
573 |       case Token.BITNOT:
574 |       case Token.BITXOR:
575 |       case Token.CALL:
576 |       case Token.CAST:
577 |       case Token.COMMA:
578 |       case Token.DEC:
579 |       case Token.DELPROP:
580 |       case Token.DIV:
581 |       case Token.EQ:
582 |       case Token.FALSE:
583 |       case Token.GE:
584 |       case Token.GETPROP:
585 |       case Token.GETELEM:
586 |       case Token.GT:
587 |       case Token.HOOK:
588 |       case Token.IN:
589 |       case Token.INC:
590 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
591 |       case Token.LE:
592 |       case Token.LSH:
593 |       case Token.LT:
594 |       case Token.MOD:
595 |       case Token.MUL:
596 |       case Token.NAME:

===== M0232 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 935-994
Documentation:

Method code:
935 |   private void toString(
936 |       StringBuilder sb,
937 |       boolean printSource,
938 |       boolean printAnnotations,
939 |       boolean printType) {
940 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
941 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
942 |       sb.append(' ');
943 |       sb.append(getString());
944 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
945 |       sb.append(' ');
946 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
947 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
948 |       // toStringTree.
949 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
950 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
951 |       } else {
952 |         sb.append(first.getString());
953 |       }
954 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getDouble());
957 |     }
958 |     if (printSource) {
959 |       int lineno = getLineno();
960 |       if (lineno != -1) {
961 |         sb.append(' ');
962 |         sb.append(lineno);
963 |       }
964 |     }
965 | 
966 |     if (printAnnotations) {
967 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
968 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
969 |         int type = keys[i];
970 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
971 |         sb.append(" [");
972 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
973 |         sb.append(": ");
974 |         String value;
975 |         switch (type) {
976 |           default:
977 |             value = x.toString();
978 |             break;
979 |         }
980 |         sb.append(value);
981 |         sb.append(']');
982 |       }
983 |     }
984 | 
985 |     if (printType) {
986 |       if (jsType != null) {
987 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
988 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
989 |           sb.append(" : ");
990 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
991 |         }
992 |       }
993 |     }
994 |   }

===== M0233 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 490-507
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
490 |     @Override
491 |     void maybeCutLine() {
492 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
493 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
494 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
495 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
496 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
497 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
498 |           prevCutPosition = position;
499 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
500 |           lineIndex++;
501 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
502 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
503 |         } else {
504 |           startNewLine();
505 |         }
506 |       }
507 |     }

===== M0258 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1944-1946
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1944 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1945 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1946 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
534 |         return true;
535 |       }
536 |     }
537 |     return false;
538 |   }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 563-631
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
563 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
564 |     if (this == that) {
565 |       return true;
566 |     }
567 | 
568 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
569 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
570 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
571 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
572 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
573 |         // with everyone.
574 |         return true;
575 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
576 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
577 |         // both unknown.
578 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
579 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
580 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
581 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
582 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
583 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
584 |         return false;
585 |       }
586 |     }
587 | 
588 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
589 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
590 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
591 |     }
592 | 
593 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
594 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
595 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
596 |     }
597 | 
598 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
599 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
600 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
601 |     }
602 | 
603 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
604 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
605 |       return false;
606 |     }
607 | 
608 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
609 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
610 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
611 |     }
612 | 
613 |     // Unbox other proxies.
614 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
615 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
616 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
617 |               that, eqMethod);
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
621 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
622 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
623 |           eqMethod);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
627 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
628 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
629 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
630 |     return this == that;
631 |   }

===== M0276 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }
