===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 120-285
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 123-169
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the boolean value of a node that represents a literal. This method
   * effectively emulates the <code>Boolean()</code> JavaScript cast function
   * except it return UNKNOWN for known values with side-effects, use
   * getImpureBooleanValue if you don't care about side-effects.
   */
Method code:
123 |   static TernaryValue getPureBooleanValue(Node n) {
124 |     switch (n.getType()) {
125 |       case Token.STRING:
126 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getString().length() > 0);
127 | 
128 |       case Token.NUMBER:
129 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getDouble() != 0);
130 | 
131 |       case Token.NOT:
132 |         return getPureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild()).not();
133 | 
134 |       case Token.NULL:
135 |       case Token.FALSE:
136 |         return TernaryValue.FALSE;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.VOID:
139 |         if (!mayHaveSideEffects(n.getFirstChild())) {
140 |           return TernaryValue.FALSE;
141 |         }
142 |         break;
143 | 
144 |       case Token.NAME:
145 |         String name = n.getString();
146 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
147 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
148 |           // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
149 |           // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
150 |           return TernaryValue.FALSE;
151 |         } else if ("Infinity".equals(name)) {
152 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
153 |         }
154 |         break;
155 | 
156 |       case Token.TRUE:
157 |       case Token.REGEXP:
158 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
161 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
162 |         if (!mayHaveSideEffects(n)) {
163 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
164 |         }
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
169 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryMinimizeCondition(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 1129-1309
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to minimize conditions expressions, as there are additional
   * assumptions that can be made when it is known that the final result
   * is a boolean.
   *
   * The following transformations are done recursively:
   *   !(x||y) --> !x&&!y
   *   !(x&&y) --> !x||!y
   *   !!x     --> x
   * Thus:
   *   !(x&&!y) --> !x||!!y --> !x||y
   *
   *   Returns the replacement for n, or the original if no change was made
   */
Method code:
1129 |   private Node tryMinimizeCondition(Node n) {
1130 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1131 | 
1132 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1133 |       case Token.NOT:
1134 |         Node first = n.getFirstChild();
1135 |         switch (first.getType()) {
1136 |           case Token.NOT: {
1137 |               Node newRoot = first.removeFirstChild();
1138 |               parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
1139 |               reportCodeChange();
1140 |               // No need to traverse, tryMinimizeCondition is called on the
1141 |               // NOT children are handled below.
1142 |               return newRoot;
1143 |             }
1144 |           case Token.AND:
1145 |           case Token.OR: {
1146 |               // !(!x && !y) --> x || y
1147 |               // !(!x || !y) --> x && y
1148 |               // !(!x && y) --> x || !y
1149 |               // !(!x || y) --> x && !y
1150 |               // !(x && !y) --> !x || y
1151 |               // !(x || !y) --> !x && y
1152 |               // !(x && y) --> !x || !y
1153 |               // !(x || y) --> !x && !y
1154 |               Node leftParent = first.getFirstChild();
1155 |               Node rightParent = first.getLastChild();
1156 |               Node left, right;
1157 | 
1158 |               // Check special case when such transformation cannot reduce
1159 |               // due to the added ()
1160 |               // It only occurs when both of expressions are not NOT expressions
1161 |               if (!leftParent.isNot()
1162 |                   && !rightParent.isNot()) {
1163 |                 // If an expression has higher precedence than && or ||,
1164 |                 // but lower precedence than NOT, an additional () is needed
1165 |                 // Thus we do not preceed
1166 |                 int op_precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType());
1167 |                 if ((isLowerPrecedence(leftParent, NOT_PRECEDENCE)
1168 |                     && isHigherPrecedence(leftParent, op_precedence))
1169 |                     || (isLowerPrecedence(rightParent, NOT_PRECEDENCE)
1170 |                     && isHigherPrecedence(rightParent, op_precedence))) {
1171 |                   return n;
1172 |                 }
1173 |               }
1174 | 
1175 |               if (leftParent.isNot()) {
1176 |                 left = leftParent.removeFirstChild();
1177 |               } else {
1178 |                 leftParent.detachFromParent();
1179 |                 left = IR.not(leftParent).srcref(leftParent);
1180 |               }
1181 |               if (rightParent.isNot()) {
1182 |                 right = rightParent.removeFirstChild();
1183 |               } else {
1184 |                 rightParent.detachFromParent();
1185 |                 right = IR.not(rightParent).srcref(rightParent);
1186 |               }
1187 | 
1188 |               int newOp = (first.isAnd()) ? Token.OR : Token.AND;
1189 |               Node newRoot = new Node(newOp, left, right);
1190 |               parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
1191 |               reportCodeChange();
1192 |               // No need to traverse, tryMinimizeCondition is called on the
1193 |               // AND and OR children below.
1194 |               return newRoot;
1195 |             }
1196 | 
1197 |            default:
1198 |              TernaryValue nVal = NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(first);
1199 |              if (nVal != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
1200 |                boolean result = nVal.not().toBoolean(true);
1201 |                int equivalentResult = result ? 1 : 0;
1202 |                return maybeReplaceChildWithNumber(n, parent, equivalentResult);
1203 |              }
1204 |         }
1205 |         // No need to traverse, tryMinimizeCondition is called on the NOT
1206 |         // children in the general case in the main post-order traversal.
1207 |         return n;
1208 | 
1209 |       case Token.OR:
1210 |       case Token.AND: {
1211 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
1212 |         Node right = n.getLastChild();
1213 | 
1214 |         // Because the expression is in a boolean context minimize
1215 |         // the children, this can't be done in the general case.
1216 |         left = tryMinimizeCondition(left);
1217 |         right = tryMinimizeCondition(right);
1218 | 
1219 |         // Remove useless conditionals
1220 |         // Handle four cases:
1221 |         //   x || false --> x
1222 |         //   x || true  --> true
1223 |         //   x && true --> x
1224 |         //   x && false  --> false
1225 |         TernaryValue rightVal = NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(right);
1226 |         if (NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(right) != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
1227 |           int type = n.getType();
1228 |           Node replacement = null;
1229 |           boolean rval = rightVal.toBoolean(true);
1230 | 
1231 |           // (x || FALSE) => x
1232 |           // (x && TRUE) => x
1233 |           if (type == Token.OR && !rval ||
1234 |               type == Token.AND && rval) {
1235 |             replacement = left;
1236 |           } else if (!mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
1237 |             replacement = right;
1238 |           }
1239 | 
1240 |           if (replacement != null) {
1241 |             n.detachChildren();
1242 |             parent.replaceChild(n, replacement);
1243 |             reportCodeChange();
1244 |             return replacement;
1245 |           }
1246 |         }
1247 |         return n;
1248 |       }
1249 | 
1250 |       case Token.HOOK: {
1251 |         Node condition = n.getFirstChild();
1252 |         Node trueNode = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1253 |         Node falseNode = n.getLastChild();
1254 | 
1255 |         // Because the expression is in a boolean context minimize
1256 |         // the result children, this can't be done in the general case.
1257 |         // The condition is handled in the general case in #optimizeSubtree
1258 |         trueNode = tryMinimizeCondition(trueNode);
1259 |         falseNode = tryMinimizeCondition(falseNode);
1260 | 
1261 |         // Handle four cases:
1262 |         //   x ? true : false --> x
1263 |         //   x ? false : true --> !x
1264 |         //   x ? true : y     --> x || y
1265 |         //   x ? y : false    --> x && y
1266 |         Node replacement = null;
1267 |         TernaryValue trueNodeVal = NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(trueNode);
1268 |         TernaryValue falseNodeVal = NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(falseNode);
1269 |         if (trueNodeVal == TernaryValue.TRUE
1270 |             && falseNodeVal == TernaryValue.FALSE) {
1271 |           // Remove useless conditionals, keep the condition
1272 |           condition.detachFromParent();
1273 |           replacement = condition;
1274 |         } else if (trueNodeVal == TernaryValue.FALSE
1275 |             && falseNodeVal == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
1276 |           // Remove useless conditionals, keep the condition
1277 |           condition.detachFromParent();
1278 |           replacement = IR.not(condition);
1279 |         } else if (trueNodeVal == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
1280 |           // Remove useless true case.
1281 |           n.detachChildren();
1282 |           replacement = IR.or(condition, falseNode);
1283 |         } else if (falseNodeVal == TernaryValue.FALSE) {
1284 |           // Remove useless false case
1285 |           n.detachChildren();
1286 |           replacement = IR.and(condition, trueNode);
1287 |         }
1288 | 
1289 |         if (replacement != null) {
1290 |           parent.replaceChild(n, replacement);
1291 |           n = replacement;
1292 |           reportCodeChange();
1293 |         }
1294 | 
1295 |         return n;
1296 |       }
1297 | 
1298 |       default:
1299 |         // while(true) --> while(1)
1300 |         TernaryValue nVal = NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(n);
1301 |         if (nVal != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
1302 |           boolean result = nVal.toBoolean(true);
1303 |           int equivalentResult = result ? 1 : 0;
1304 |           return maybeReplaceChildWithNumber(n, parent, equivalentResult);
1305 |         }
1306 |         // We can't do anything else currently.
1307 |         return n;
1308 |     }
1309 |   }

===== M0310 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.optimizeSubtree(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 78-169
Documentation:
/**
   * Tries apply our various peephole minimizations on the passed in node.
   */
Method code:
78 |   @Override
79 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
80 |   public Node optimizeSubtree(Node node) {
81 |     switch(node.getType()) {
82 |       case Token.RETURN: {
83 |         Node result = tryRemoveRedundantExit(node);
84 |         if (result != node) {
85 |           return result;
86 |         }
87 |         result = tryReplaceExitWithBreak(node);
88 |         if (result != node) {
89 |           return result;
90 |         }
91 |         return tryReduceReturn(node);
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       case Token.THROW: {
95 |         Node result = tryRemoveRedundantExit(node);
96 |         if (result != node) {
97 |           return result;
98 |         }
99 |         return tryReplaceExitWithBreak(node);
100 |       }
101 | 
102 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe remove redundant BREAK and CONTINUE. Overlaps
103 |       // with MinimizeExitPoints.
104 | 
105 |       case Token.NOT:
106 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
107 |         return tryMinimizeNot(node);
108 | 
109 |       case Token.IF:
110 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
111 |         return tryMinimizeIf(node);
112 | 
113 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
114 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
115 |         return node;
116 | 
117 |       case Token.HOOK:
118 |         tryMinimizeCondition(node.getFirstChild());
119 |         return node;
120 | 
121 |       case Token.WHILE:
122 |       case Token.DO:
123 |         tryMinimizeCondition(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
124 |         return node;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.FOR:
127 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(node)) {
128 |           tryJoinForCondition(node);
129 |           tryMinimizeCondition(NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(node));
130 |         }
131 |         return node;
132 | 
133 |       case Token.TRUE:
134 |       case Token.FALSE:
135 |         return reduceTrueFalse(node);
136 | 
137 |       case Token.NEW:
138 |         node = tryFoldStandardConstructors(node);
139 |         if (!node.isCall()) {
140 |           return node;
141 |         }
142 |         // Fall through on purpose because tryFoldStandardConstructors() may
143 |         // convert a NEW node into a CALL node
144 |       case Token.CALL:
145 |         Node result =  tryFoldLiteralConstructor(node);
146 |         if (result == node) {
147 |           result = tryFoldSimpleFunctionCall(node);
148 |           if (result == node) {
149 |             result = tryFoldImmediateCallToBoundFunction(node);
150 |           }
151 |         }
152 |         return result;
153 | 
154 |       case Token.COMMA:
155 |         return trySplitComma(node);
156 | 
157 |       case Token.NAME:
158 |         return tryReplaceUndefined(node);
159 | 
160 |       case Token.BLOCK:
161 |         return tryReplaceIf(node);
162 | 
163 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
164 |         return tryMinimizeArrayLiteral(node);
165 | 
166 |       default:
167 |         return node; //Nothing changed
168 |     }
169 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 502-518

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
502 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
503 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
504 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
505 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
506 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
507 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
508 |         String inputName = input.getName();
509 | 
510 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
511 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
512 |         // will get an error down the line.
513 |         inputs.add(input);
514 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
515 |       }
516 |     }
517 |     return inputs;
518 |   }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1229-1273
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1229 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1230 |     // unknown
1231 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1232 |       return true;
1233 |     }
1234 |     // all type
1235 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1236 |       return true;
1237 |     }
1238 |     // equality
1239 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1240 |       return true;
1241 |     }
1242 |     // unions
1243 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1244 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1245 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1246 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1247 |           return true;
1248 |         }
1249 |       }
1250 |       return false;
1251 |     }
1252 | 
1253 |     // templatized types.
1254 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1255 |       return !areIncompatibleArrays(thisType, thatType) &&
1256 |           thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1257 |               thatType);
1258 |     }
1259 |     if (thatType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1260 |       if (!isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType) &&
1261 |           !thisType.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
1262 |               thatType.getTemplateTypeMap(), EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
1263 |         return false;
1264 |       }
1265 |     }
1266 | 
1267 |     // proxy types
1268 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1269 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1270 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1271 |     }
1272 |     return false;
1273 |   }

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryMinimizeIf(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 636-868
Documentation:
/**
   * Try turning IF nodes into smaller HOOKs
   *
   * Returns the replacement for n or the original if no replacement was
   * necessary.
   */
Method code:
636 |   private Node tryMinimizeIf(Node n) {
637 | 
638 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
639 | 
640 |     Node cond = n.getFirstChild();
641 | 
642 |     /* If the condition is a literal, we'll let other
643 |      * optimizations try to remove useless code.
644 |      */
645 |     if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(cond, true)) {
646 |       return n;
647 |     }
648 | 
649 |     Node thenBranch = cond.getNext();
650 |     Node elseBranch = thenBranch.getNext();
651 | 
652 |     if (elseBranch == null) {
653 |       if (isFoldableExpressBlock(thenBranch)) {
654 |         Node expr = getBlockExpression(thenBranch);
655 |         if (!late && isPropertyAssignmentInExpression(expr)) {
656 |           // Keep opportunities for CollapseProperties such as
657 |           // a.longIdentifier || a.longIdentifier = ... -> var a = ...;
658 |           // until CollapseProperties has been run.
659 |           return n;
660 |         }
661 | 
662 |         if (cond.isNot()) {
663 |           // if(!x)bar(); -> x||bar();
664 |           if (isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(cond, OR_PRECEDENCE) &&
665 |               isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(expr.getFirstChild(),
666 |                   OR_PRECEDENCE)) {
667 |             // It's not okay to add two sets of parentheses.
668 |             return n;
669 |           }
670 | 
671 |           Node or = IR.or(
672 |               cond.removeFirstChild(),
673 |               expr.removeFirstChild()).srcref(n);
674 |           Node newExpr = NodeUtil.newExpr(or);
675 |           parent.replaceChild(n, newExpr);
676 |           reportCodeChange();
677 | 
678 |           return newExpr;
679 |         }
680 | 
681 |         // if(x)foo(); -> x&&foo();
682 |         if (isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(cond, AND_PRECEDENCE) &&
683 |             isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(expr.getFirstChild(),
684 |                 AND_PRECEDENCE)) {
685 |           // One additional set of parentheses is worth the change even if
686 |           // there is no immediate code size win. However, two extra pair of
687 |           // {}, we would have to think twice. (unless we know for sure the
688 |           // we can further optimize its parent.
689 |           return n;
690 |         }
691 | 
692 |         n.removeChild(cond);
693 |         Node and = IR.and(cond, expr.removeFirstChild()).srcref(n);
694 |         Node newExpr = NodeUtil.newExpr(and);
695 |         parent.replaceChild(n, newExpr);
696 |         reportCodeChange();
697 | 
698 |         return newExpr;
699 |       } else {
700 | 
701 |         // Try to combine two IF-ELSE
702 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(thenBranch) &&
703 |             thenBranch.hasOneChild()) {
704 |           Node innerIf = thenBranch.getFirstChild();
705 | 
706 |           if (innerIf.isIf()) {
707 |             Node innerCond = innerIf.getFirstChild();
708 |             Node innerThenBranch = innerCond.getNext();
709 |             Node innerElseBranch = innerThenBranch.getNext();
710 | 
711 |             if (innerElseBranch == null &&
712 |                  !(isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(cond, AND_PRECEDENCE) &&
713 |                    isLowerPrecedenceInExpression(innerCond, AND_PRECEDENCE))) {
714 |               n.detachChildren();
715 |               n.addChildToBack(
716 |                   IR.and(
717 |                       cond,
718 |                       innerCond.detachFromParent())
719 |                       .srcref(cond));
720 |               n.addChildrenToBack(innerThenBranch.detachFromParent());
721 |               reportCodeChange();
722 |               // Not worth trying to fold the current IF-ELSE into && because
723 |               // the inner IF-ELSE wasn't able to be folded into && anyways.
724 |               return n;
725 |             }
726 |           }
727 |         }
728 |       }
729 | 
730 |       return n;
731 |     }
732 | 
733 |     /* TODO(dcc) This modifies the siblings of n, which is undesirable for a
734 |      * peephole optimization. This should probably get moved to another pass.
735 |      */
736 |     tryRemoveRepeatedStatements(n);
737 | 
738 |     // if(!x)foo();else bar(); -> if(x)bar();else foo();
739 |     // An additional set of curly braces isn't worth it.
740 |     if (cond.isNot() && !consumesDanglingElse(elseBranch)) {
741 |       n.replaceChild(cond, cond.removeFirstChild());
742 |       n.removeChild(thenBranch);
743 |       n.addChildToBack(thenBranch);
744 |       reportCodeChange();
745 |       return n;
746 |     }
747 | 
748 |     // if(x)return 1;else return 2; -> return x?1:2;
749 |     if (isReturnExpressBlock(thenBranch) && isReturnExpressBlock(elseBranch)) {
750 |       Node thenExpr = getBlockReturnExpression(thenBranch);
751 |       Node elseExpr = getBlockReturnExpression(elseBranch);
752 |       n.removeChild(cond);
753 |       thenExpr.detachFromParent();
754 |       elseExpr.detachFromParent();
755 | 
756 |       // note - we ignore any cases with "return;", technically this
757 |       // can be converted to "return undefined;" or some variant, but
758 |       // that does not help code size.
759 |       Node returnNode = IR.returnNode(
760 |                             IR.hook(cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
761 |                                 .srcref(n));
762 |       parent.replaceChild(n, returnNode);
763 |       reportCodeChange();
764 |       return returnNode;
765 |     }
766 | 
767 |     boolean thenBranchIsExpressionBlock = isFoldableExpressBlock(thenBranch);
768 |     boolean elseBranchIsExpressionBlock = isFoldableExpressBlock(elseBranch);
769 | 
770 |     if (thenBranchIsExpressionBlock && elseBranchIsExpressionBlock) {
771 |       Node thenOp = getBlockExpression(thenBranch).getFirstChild();
772 |       Node elseOp = getBlockExpression(elseBranch).getFirstChild();
773 |       if (thenOp.getType() == elseOp.getType()) {
774 |         // if(x)a=1;else a=2; -> a=x?1:2;
775 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(thenOp)) {
776 |           Node lhs = thenOp.getFirstChild();
777 |           if (areNodesEqualForInlining(lhs, elseOp.getFirstChild()) &&
778 |               // if LHS has side effects, don't proceed [since the optimization
779 |               // evaluates LHS before cond]
780 |               // NOTE - there are some circumstances where we can
781 |               // proceed even if there are side effects...
782 |               !mayEffectMutableState(lhs)) {
783 | 
784 |             n.removeChild(cond);
785 |             Node assignName = thenOp.removeFirstChild();
786 |             Node thenExpr = thenOp.removeFirstChild();
787 |             Node elseExpr = elseOp.getLastChild();
788 |             elseOp.removeChild(elseExpr);
789 | 
790 |             Node hookNode = IR.hook(cond, thenExpr, elseExpr).srcref(n);
791 |             Node assign = new Node(thenOp.getType(), assignName, hookNode)
792 |                               .srcref(thenOp);
793 |             Node expr = NodeUtil.newExpr(assign);
794 |             parent.replaceChild(n, expr);
795 |             reportCodeChange();
796 | 
797 |             return expr;
798 |           }
799 |         }
800 |       }
801 |       // if(x)foo();else bar(); -> x?foo():bar()
802 |       n.removeChild(cond);
803 |       thenOp.detachFromParent();
804 |       elseOp.detachFromParent();
805 |       Node expr = IR.exprResult(
806 |           IR.hook(cond, thenOp, elseOp).srcref(n));
807 |       parent.replaceChild(n, expr);
808 |       reportCodeChange();
809 |       return expr;
810 |     }
811 | 
812 |     boolean thenBranchIsVar = isVarBlock(thenBranch);
813 |     boolean elseBranchIsVar = isVarBlock(elseBranch);
814 | 
815 |     // if(x)var y=1;else y=2  ->  var y=x?1:2
816 |     if (thenBranchIsVar && elseBranchIsExpressionBlock &&
817 |         getBlockExpression(elseBranch).getFirstChild().isAssign()) {
818 | 
819 |       Node var = getBlockVar(thenBranch);
820 |       Node elseAssign = getBlockExpression(elseBranch).getFirstChild();
821 | 
822 |       Node name1 = var.getFirstChild();
823 |       Node maybeName2 = elseAssign.getFirstChild();
824 | 
825 |       if (name1.hasChildren()
826 |           && maybeName2.isName()
827 |           && name1.getString().equals(maybeName2.getString())) {
828 |         Node thenExpr = name1.removeChildren();
829 |         Node elseExpr = elseAssign.getLastChild().detachFromParent();
830 |         cond.detachFromParent();
831 |         Node hookNode = IR.hook(cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
832 |                             .srcref(n);
833 |         var.detachFromParent();
834 |         name1.addChildrenToBack(hookNode);
835 |         parent.replaceChild(n, var);
836 |         reportCodeChange();
837 |         return var;
838 |       }
839 | 
840 |     // if(x)y=1;else var y=2  ->  var y=x?1:2
841 |     } else if (elseBranchIsVar && thenBranchIsExpressionBlock &&
842 |         getBlockExpression(thenBranch).getFirstChild().isAssign()) {
843 | 
844 |       Node var = getBlockVar(elseBranch);
845 |       Node thenAssign = getBlockExpression(thenBranch).getFirstChild();
846 | 
847 |       Node maybeName1 = thenAssign.getFirstChild();
848 |       Node name2 = var.getFirstChild();
849 | 
850 |       if (name2.hasChildren()
851 |           && maybeName1.isName()
852 |           && maybeName1.getString().equals(name2.getString())) {
853 |         Node thenExpr = thenAssign.getLastChild().detachFromParent();
854 |         Node elseExpr = name2.removeChildren();
855 |         cond.detachFromParent();
856 |         Node hookNode = IR.hook(cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
857 |                             .srcref(n);
858 |         var.detachFromParent();
859 |         name2.addChildrenToBack(hookNode);
860 |         parent.replaceChild(n, var);
861 |         reportCodeChange();
862 | 
863 |         return var;
864 |       }
865 |     }
866 | 
867 |     return n;
868 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.NumberNode.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 166-178
Documentation:

Method code:
166 |     @Override
167 |     boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
168 |       boolean equivalent = super.isEquivalentTo(node, compareJsType, recurse);
169 |       if (equivalent) {
170 |         double thisValue = getDouble();
171 |         double thatValue = ((NumberNode) node).getDouble();
172 |         if (thisValue == thatValue) {
173 |           // detect the difference between 0.0 and -0.0.
174 |           return (thisValue != 0.0) || (1/thisValue == 1/thatValue);
175 |         }
176 |       }
177 |       return false;
178 |     }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 440-475
Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
440 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
441 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
442 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
443 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
444 | 
445 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
446 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
447 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
448 | 
449 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
450 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
451 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
452 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
453 |     }
454 | 
455 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
456 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
457 |       if (superClass != null) {
458 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
459 |       }
460 | 
461 |       if (isInterface()) {
462 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
463 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
464 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
465 |           }
466 |         }
467 |       }
468 |     }
469 | 
470 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
471 |       clearCachedValues();
472 |     }
473 | 
474 |     return true;
475 |   }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2429-2438
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2429 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2430 |     int len = s.length();
2431 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2432 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2433 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2434 |         return false;
2435 |       }
2436 |     }
2437 |     return true;
2438 |   }

===== M0381 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

/**

   *        which might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.

===== M0389 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 587-627
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured variables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
587 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
588 |     switch (n.getType()) {
589 |       case Token.CAST:
590 |         return isLiteralValue(n.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions);
591 | 
592 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
593 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
594 |              child = child.getNext()) {
595 |           if ((!child.isEmpty()) && !isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
596 |             return false;
597 |           }
598 |         }
599 |         return true;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.REGEXP:
602 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
603 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
604 |              child = child.getNext()) {
605 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
606 |             return false;
607 |           }
608 |         }
609 |         return true;
610 | 
611 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
612 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
613 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
614 |              child = child.getNext()) {
615 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
616 |             return false;
617 |           }
618 |         }
619 |         return true;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
622 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
623 | 
624 |       default:
625 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
626 |     }
627 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 137-154
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
137 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
138 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
139 |     int thisNumKeys = templateKeys.size();
140 |     int thatNumKeys = that.getTemplateKeys().size();
141 | 
142 |     for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(thisNumKeys, thatNumKeys); i++) {
143 |       JSType thisTemplateType = getTemplateType(templateKeys.get(i));
144 |       JSType thatTemplateType = that.getTemplateType(
145 |           that.getTemplateKeys().get(i));
146 |       if (!thisTemplateType.checkEquivalenceHelper(
147 |           thatTemplateType, eqMethod)) {
148 |         return false;
149 |       }
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     return thisNumKeys == thatNumKeys ||
153 |         eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT;
154 |   }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 490-514
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
490 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
491 |     switch (n.getType()) {
492 |       case Token.STRING:
493 |       case Token.NUMBER:
494 |       case Token.NULL:
495 |       case Token.TRUE:
496 |       case Token.FALSE:
497 |         return true;
498 |       case Token.CAST:
499 |       case Token.NOT:
500 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
501 |       case Token.VOID:
502 |       case Token.NEG:
503 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
504 |       case Token.NAME:
505 |         String name = n.getString();
506 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
507 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
508 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
509 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
510 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
511 |     }
512 | 
513 |     return false;
514 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1695-1709
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1695 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1696 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1697 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1698 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1699 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1700 |       if (result.last != null) {
1701 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1702 |       }
1703 |       if (result.first == null) {
1704 |         result.first = n2clone;
1705 |       }
1706 |       result.last = n2clone;
1707 |     }
1708 |     return result;
1709 |   }

===== M0452 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 596-611
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
596 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
597 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
598 |       Set<String> directives = null;
599 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
600 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
601 |         if (directives == null) {
602 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
603 |         } else {
604 |           directives.add(directive);
605 |         }
606 |       }
607 | 
608 |       if (directives != null) {
609 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
610 |       }
611 |     }

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 310-349
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
310 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
311 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
312 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
313 |       options.checkTypes = true;
314 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
315 |       options.checkTypes = false;
316 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
317 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
318 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
319 |       // parser type warnings.
320 |       options.setWarningLevel(
321 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
322 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
323 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
324 |     }
325 | 
326 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
327 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
328 |       options.setWarningLevel(
329 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
330 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
331 |     }
332 | 
333 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
334 |       options.setWarningLevel(
335 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
336 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
337 |     }
338 | 
339 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
340 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
341 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
342 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
343 |     // error off.
344 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
345 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
346 |       options.setWarningLevel(
347 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
348 |     }
349 |   }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

===== M0470 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 765-781
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
765 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
766 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
767 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
768 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
769 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
770 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
774 | 
775 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
776 |     do {
777 |       current = current.getParent();
778 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
779 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
780 |     } while (current != n);
781 |   }

===== M0481 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 752-834
Documentation:

Method code:
752 |     @Override
753 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
754 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
755 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
756 |       if (name == null) {
757 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
758 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
759 |           errorReporter.error(
760 |             "unnamed function statement",
761 |             sourceName,
762 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
763 | 
764 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
765 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
766 |         }
767 |         name = new Name();
768 |         name.setIdentifier("");
769 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
770 |       }
771 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
772 |       Node newName = transform(name);
773 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
774 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
775 |         // declaration.
776 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
777 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
778 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
779 |         // same line as the function keyword.
780 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
781 |             functionNode.getLp();
782 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
783 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
784 |       }
785 | 
786 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
787 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
788 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
789 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
790 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
791 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
792 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
793 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
794 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
795 |       if (fnName != null) {
796 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
797 |       } else {
798 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
799 |       }
800 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
801 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
802 | 
803 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
804 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
805 |         Node paramNode = transformParameter(param);
806 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
807 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
808 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
809 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
810 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
811 |         } else {
812 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
813 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
814 |           // (an error has already been reported).
815 |           Preconditions.checkState(
816 |               config.isIdeMode
817 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
818 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
819 |         }
820 |       }
821 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
822 | 
823 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
824 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
825 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
826 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
827 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
828 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
829 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
830 |       }
831 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
832 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
833 |      return node;
834 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1431 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1432 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1433 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1434 |     }
1435 | 
1436 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1437 |     Node n, n2;
1438 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1439 |          res == null && n != null;
1440 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1441 |       if (node2 == null) {
1442 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1443 |       }
1444 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1445 |       if (res != null) {
1446 |         return res;
1447 |       }
1448 |     }
1449 |     return res;
1450 |   }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 549-569
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
549 |   @Override
550 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
551 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
552 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
553 |     if (unknown) {
554 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
555 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
556 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
557 |         unknown = false;
558 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
559 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
560 |             unknown = true;
561 |             break;
562 |           }
563 |         }
564 |       } else {
565 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
566 |       }
567 |     }
568 |     return unknown;
569 |   }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.replaceChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 704-725
Documentation:
/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
704 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
705 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
706 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
707 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
708 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
709 | 
710 |     // Copy over important information.
711 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
712 | 
713 |     newChild.next = child.next;
714 |     newChild.parent = this;
715 |     if (child == first) {
716 |         first = newChild;
717 |     } else {
718 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
719 |         prev.next = newChild;
720 |     }
721 |     if (child == last)
722 |         last = newChild;
723 |     child.next = null;
724 |     child.parent = null;
725 |   }

===== M0507 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.traverse(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 120-145
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   private void traverse(Node node) {
121 |     // The goal here is to avoid retraversing
122 |     // the entire AST to catch newly created opportunities.
123 |     // So we track whether a "unit of code" has changed,
124 |     // and revisit immediately.
125 |     if (!shouldVisit(node)) {
126 |       return;
127 |     }
128 | 
129 |     int visits = 0;
130 |     do {
131 |       Node c = node.getFirstChild();
132 |       while (c != null) {
133 |         Node next = c.getNext();
134 |         traverse(c);
135 |         c = next;
136 |       }
137 | 
138 |       visit(node);
139 |       visits++;
140 | 
141 |       Preconditions.checkState(visits < 10000, "too many interations");
142 |     } while (shouldRetraverse(node));
143 | 
144 |     exitNode(node);
145 |   }

===== M0523 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 514-542
Documentation:

Method code:
514 |     @Override
515 |     void endFile() {
516 |       super.endFile();
517 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
518 |         return;
519 |       }
520 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
521 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
522 |         append(";");
523 |         startNewLine();
524 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
525 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
526 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
527 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
528 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
529 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
530 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
531 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
532 |         lineIndex--;
533 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
534 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
535 |         append(";");
536 |         startNewLine();
537 |       } else {
538 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
539 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
540 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
541 |       }
542 |     }

===== M0538 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1421-1457
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1421 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1422 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1423 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1424 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1425 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1426 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1427 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1428 |           continue;
1429 |         }
1430 |       }
1431 | 
1432 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1433 | 
1434 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1435 |       if (n == null) {
1436 |         continue;
1437 |       }
1438 | 
1439 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1440 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1441 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1442 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1443 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1444 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1445 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1446 | 
1447 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1448 | 
1449 |         externs.add(input);
1450 |         staleInputs = true;
1451 |       }
1452 |     }
1453 | 
1454 |     if (staleInputs) {
1455 |       repartitionInputs();
1456 |     }
1457 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.

106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

1147 |     @Override
1148 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
1149 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
1150 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
1151 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.isNumber()) {
1152 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());

===== M0558 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryRemoveRepeatedStatements(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 892-920
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to remove duplicate statements from IF blocks. For example:
   *
   * if (a) {
   *   x = 1;
   *   return true;
   * } else {
   *   x = 2;
   *   return true;
   * }
   *
   * becomes:
   *
   * if (a) {
   *   x = 1;
   * } else {
   *   x = 2;
   * }
   * return true;
   *
   * @param n The IF node to examine.
   */
Method code:
892 |   private void tryRemoveRepeatedStatements(Node n) {
893 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isIf());
894 | 
895 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
896 |     if (!NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)) {
897 |       // If the immediate parent is something like a label, we
898 |       // can't move the statement, so bail.
899 |       return;
900 |     }
901 | 
902 |     Node cond = n.getFirstChild();
903 |     Node trueBranch = cond.getNext();
904 |     Node falseBranch = trueBranch.getNext();
905 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(trueBranch);
906 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(falseBranch);
907 | 
908 |     while (true) {
909 |       Node lastTrue = trueBranch.getLastChild();
910 |       Node lastFalse = falseBranch.getLastChild();
911 |       if (lastTrue == null || lastFalse == null
912 |           || !areNodesEqualForInlining(lastTrue, lastFalse)) {
913 |         break;
914 |       }
915 |       lastTrue.detachFromParent();
916 |       lastFalse.detachFromParent();
917 |       parent.addChildAfter(lastTrue, n);
918 |       reportCodeChange();
919 |     }
920 |   }

910 |       add("(");
911 |       add(n, Context.OTHER);
912 |       add(")");
913 |     } else {
914 |       add(n, context);
915 |     }
916 |   }

83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());

87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.isFoldableExpressBlock(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 926-957
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the node is a block with a single statement that is
   *     an expression.
   */
Method code:
926 |   private boolean isFoldableExpressBlock(Node n) {
927 |     if (n.isBlock()) {
928 |       if (n.hasOneChild()) {
929 |         Node maybeExpr = n.getFirstChild();
930 |         if (maybeExpr.isExprResult()) {
931 |           // IE has a bug where event handlers behave differently when
932 |           // their return value is used vs. when their return value is in
933 |           // an EXPR_RESULT. It's pretty freaking weird. See:
934 |           // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=291
935 |           // We try to detect this case, and not fold EXPR_RESULTs
936 |           // into other expressions.
937 |           if (maybeExpr.getFirstChild().isCall()) {
938 |             Node calledFn = maybeExpr.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
939 | 
940 |             // We only have to worry about methods with an implicit 'this'
941 |             // param, or this doesn't happen.
942 |             if (calledFn.isGetElem()) {
943 |               return false;
944 |             } else if (calledFn.isGetProp() &&
945 |                        calledFn.getLastChild().getString().startsWith("on")) {
946 |               return false;
947 |             }
948 |           }
949 | 
950 |           return true;
951 |         }
952 |         return false;
953 |       }
954 |     }
955 | 
956 |     return false;
957 |   }

===== M0590 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 789-934
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
789 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
790 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
791 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
792 |     // that we know to be safe
793 |     switch (n.getType()) {
794 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
795 |       case Token.CAST:
796 |       case Token.AND:
797 |       case Token.BLOCK:
798 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
799 |       case Token.HOOK:
800 |       case Token.IF:
801 |       case Token.IN:
802 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
803 |       case Token.NUMBER:
804 |       case Token.OR:
805 |       case Token.THIS:
806 |       case Token.TRUE:
807 |       case Token.FALSE:
808 |       case Token.NULL:
809 |       case Token.STRING:
810 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
811 |       case Token.SWITCH:
812 |       case Token.TRY:
813 |       case Token.EMPTY:
814 |         break;
815 | 
816 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
817 |       case Token.THROW:
818 |         return true;
819 | 
820 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
821 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
822 |           return true;
823 |         }
824 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
825 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
826 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
827 |             return true;
828 |           }
829 |         }
830 |         return false;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
833 |       case Token.REGEXP:
834 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
840 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
841 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
842 |           return true;
843 |         }
844 |         break;
845 | 
846 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
847 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
848 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
849 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
850 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
851 | 
852 |       case Token.NEW:
853 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
854 |           return true;
855 |         }
856 | 
857 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
858 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
859 |           // side-effects
860 |           break;
861 |         }
862 |         return true;
863 | 
864 |       case Token.CALL:
865 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
866 |         // side effect property set.
867 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
868 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
869 |           // side-effects
870 |           break;
871 |         }
872 |         return true;
873 | 
874 |       default:
875 |         if (isSimpleOperator(n)) {
876 |           break;
877 |         }
878 | 
879 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
880 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
881 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
882 |             return true;
883 |           }
884 | 
885 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
886 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
887 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
888 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
889 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
890 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
891 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
892 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
893 |             return true;
894 |           }
895 | 
896 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
897 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
898 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
899 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
900 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
901 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
902 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
903 |               return false;
904 |             }
905 | 
906 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
907 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
908 |             // other objects.
909 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
910 |             // side-effect.
911 |             while (isGet(current)) {
912 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
913 |             }
914 | 
915 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
916 |           } else {
917 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
918 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
919 |             // preserve existing behavior.
920 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
921 |           }
922 |         }
923 | 
924 |         return true;
925 |     }
926 | 
927 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
928 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
929 |         return true;
930 |       }
931 |     }
932 | 
933 |     return false;
934 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
111 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
112 |                                   String sourceString,
113 |                                   Config config,
114 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
115 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
116 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
117 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
118 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
119 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
120 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
121 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
122 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
123 | 
124 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
125 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
126 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
127 | 
128 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
129 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
130 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
131 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
132 | 
133 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
134 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
135 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
136 | 
137 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
138 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
139 |     try {
140 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
141 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
142 |       logger.info(
143 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
144 |     } finally {
145 |       Context.exit();
146 |     }
147 |     Node root = null;
148 |     if (astRoot != null) {
149 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
150 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
151 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
152 |     }
153 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
154 |   }
