===== M0596 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1296-1416
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1296 |   Node parseInputs() {
1297 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1298 | 
1299 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1300 |     // individual file parse trees.
1301 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1302 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1303 |     }
1304 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1305 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1306 |     }
1307 | 
1308 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1309 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1310 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1311 | 
1312 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1313 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1314 | 
1315 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1316 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1317 | 
1318 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1319 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1320 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1321 |     }
1322 | 
1323 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1324 | 
1325 |     try {
1326 |       // Parse externs sources.
1327 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1328 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1329 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1330 |           return null;
1331 |         }
1332 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1333 |       }
1334 | 
1335 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1336 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1337 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1338 |       }
1339 | 
1340 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1341 | 
1342 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1343 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1344 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1345 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1346 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1347 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1348 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1349 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1350 |           }
1351 |         }
1352 | 
1353 |         try {
1354 |           inputs =
1355 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1356 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1357 |           staleInputs = true;
1358 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1359 |           report(JSError.make(
1360 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1361 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1362 |           report(JSError.make(
1363 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1364 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1365 |           report(JSError.make(
1366 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1367 |         }
1368 | 
1369 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1370 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1371 |           return null;
1372 |         }
1373 |       }
1374 | 
1375 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1376 | 
1377 |       if (staleInputs) {
1378 |         repartitionInputs();
1379 |       }
1380 | 
1381 |       // Build the AST.
1382 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1383 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1384 |         if (n == null) {
1385 |           continue;
1386 |         }
1387 | 
1388 |         if (devMode) {
1389 |           runSanityCheck();
1390 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1391 |             return null;
1392 |           }
1393 |         }
1394 | 
1395 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1396 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1397 | 
1398 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1399 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1400 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1401 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1402 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1403 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1404 |         }
1405 | 
1406 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1407 |       }
1408 | 
1409 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1410 |         return null;
1411 |       }
1412 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1413 |     } finally {
1414 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1415 |     }
1416 |   }

785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {
787 |       return null;

===== M0616 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.tryReplaceIf(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeSubstituteAlternateSyntax.java
Original range: 306-377
Documentation:
/**
   * Use "return x?1:2;" in place of "if(x)return 1;return 2;"
   */
Method code:
306 |   private Node tryReplaceIf(Node n) {
307 | 
308 |     for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
309 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()){
310 |       if (child.isIf()){
311 |         Node cond = child.getFirstChild();
312 |         Node thenBranch = cond.getNext();
313 |         Node elseBranch = thenBranch.getNext();
314 |         Node nextNode = child.getNext();
315 | 
316 |         if (nextNode != null && elseBranch == null
317 |             && isReturnBlock(thenBranch)
318 |             && nextNode.isIf()) {
319 |           Node nextCond = nextNode.getFirstChild();
320 |           Node nextThen = nextCond.getNext();
321 |           Node nextElse = nextThen.getNext();
322 |           if (thenBranch.isEquivalentToTyped(nextThen)) {
323 |             // Transform
324 |             //   if (x) return 1; if (y) return 1;
325 |             // to
326 |             //   if (x||y) return 1;
327 |             child.detachFromParent();
328 |             child.detachChildren();
329 |             Node newCond = new Node(Token.OR, cond);
330 |             nextNode.replaceChild(nextCond, newCond);
331 |             newCond.addChildToBack(nextCond);
332 |             reportCodeChange();
333 |           } else if (nextElse != null
334 |               && thenBranch.isEquivalentToTyped(nextElse)) {
335 |             // Transform
336 |             //   if (x) return 1; if (y) foo() else return 1;
337 |             // to
338 |             //   if (!x&&y) foo() else return 1;
339 |             child.detachFromParent();
340 |             child.detachChildren();
341 |             Node newCond = new Node(Token.AND,
342 |                 IR.not(cond).srcref(cond));
343 |             nextNode.replaceChild(nextCond, newCond);
344 |             newCond.addChildToBack(nextCond);
345 |             reportCodeChange();
346 |           }
347 |         } else if (nextNode != null && elseBranch == null &&
348 |             isReturnBlock(thenBranch) && isReturnExpression(nextNode)) {
349 |           Node thenExpr = null;
350 |           // if(x)return; return 1 -> return x?void 0:1
351 |           if (isReturnExpressBlock(thenBranch)) {
352 |             thenExpr = getBlockReturnExpression(thenBranch);
353 |             thenExpr.detachFromParent();
354 |           } else {
355 |             thenExpr = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(child);
356 |           }
357 | 
358 |           Node elseExpr = nextNode.getFirstChild();
359 | 
360 |           cond.detachFromParent();
361 |           elseExpr.detachFromParent();
362 | 
363 |           Node returnNode = IR.returnNode(
364 |                                 IR.hook(cond, thenExpr, elseExpr)
365 |                                     .srcref(child));
366 |           n.replaceChild(child, returnNode);
367 |           n.removeChild(nextNode);
368 |           reportCodeChange();
369 |         } else if (elseBranch != null && statementMustExitParent(thenBranch)) {
370 |           child.removeChild(elseBranch);
371 |           n.addChildAfter(elseBranch, child);
372 |           reportCodeChange();
373 |         }
374 |       }
375 |     }
376 |     return n;
377 |   }

Method code:
72 |   public CompilerInput(SourceAst ast, InputId inputId, boolean isExtern) {
73 |     this.ast = ast;
74 |     this.id = inputId;

===== M0628 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

===== M0631 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.visit(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 182-203
Documentation:

Method code:
182 |   public void visit(Node n) {
183 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
184 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
185 | 
186 |     do {
187 |       somethingChanged = false;
188 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
189 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
190 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
191 | 
192 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
193 |           somethingChanged = true;
194 | 
195 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
196 |         }
197 | 
198 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
199 |           return;
200 |         }
201 |       }
202 |     } while(somethingChanged);
203 |   }
